## Catheter Snapshots

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/tools\_cathSnapshot\_intro\_c3\_EN.xml

In some cases, it is valuable to view the previous location of one or more catheters. For example, knowing the previous location facilitates the following:

* Navigating the catheter to a previous location
* Monitoring catheter stability, through comparison to current location
* Marking a specific location, such as the PV Os

You can create up to 50 Snapshots. Three catheters are included by default (20 POLE A, 20 POLE B, and REF). You can also customize your own Snapshots. Up to six Catheter Snapshots can be displayed at a time.

![cathSnapshot_xmpl_ref-lasso.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 107: Catheter Snapshot

### Creating Catheter Snapshots

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/tools\_cathSnapshot\_create\_t\_EN.xml

A Catheter Snapshot is a record of the locations of designated catheters at a specific time during the Study. Create a Snapshot when the catheters are in a location you want to view again.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Click Catheter > Create Snapshot.  The Create Snapshot dialog box appears.  ![cathSnapshot_create_db_blank.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 2. | Enter a name for the Snapshot.  By default, the catheters connected to the 20 POLE A, 20 POLE B, and REF sockets will be added to the Snapshot. |
| 3. | To change the catheters in the Snapshot, click ![arrows_expand_down.png](data:image/png;base64...) and update the list in the expanded Create Snapshot dialog box.  ![cathSnapshot_create_db_full_changed.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 4. | Click Save. |

A Snapshot with the current locations of the selected catheters is created. The Snapshot will not be displayed until it is selected in the Snapshot Settings dialog box.

### Displaying Catheter Snapshots

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/tools\_cathSnapshot\_display\_t\_EN.xml

After a Catheter Snapshot is created, you can hide or show it.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Click Catheter > Snapshot Settings.  The Snapshot Settings dialog box appears.  ![cathSnapshot_settings_db_2.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 2. | In the Displayed Snapshot column, select the snapshot(s) to display.  Up to six Snapshots can be displayed at a time. If six are selected, you cannot select an additional Snapshot without clearing a previous selection.  ![cathSnapshot_settings_db_moreThan6.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 3. | In the Assigned Catheter column, select the catheters to display for the selected Snapshot(s).  If multiple Snapshots are selected, the Assigned Catheter settings apply to all the Snapshots. |
| 4. | To exit without saving changes, click Cancel. |
| 5. | To display the selected Snapshots, click OK. |

The Catheter Snapshots are displayed in the Main Map Viewer and Additional Map Viewer.

### Deleting Catheter Snapshots

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/tools\_cathSnapshot\_delete\_t\_EN.xml

You can remove unnecessary Catheter Snapshots.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Click Catheter > Snapshot Settings.  The Snapshot Settings dialog box appears.  ![cathSnapshot_settings_db_2.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 2. | Select the Snapshot you want to delete, and click ![btn_delete_trash.png](data:image/png;base64...) |

The Snapshot is deleted.

## Creating a Template

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/template\_create\_EN.xml

Templates are used to define layouts and initial parameters, such as the catheters which may be used for a particular type of study.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Click Study > Save as Template.  The Save as Template dialog box appears.  ![template_saveAs_db.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 2. | Select the type of study the template will be used for.  The default template and all other user-defined templates for that study type are displayed. |
| 3. | Type the name for the template in the Template Name field. |
| 4. | Click the Save button. |

The dialog box closes and the template is saved. It will be available the next time you start a study.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  To delete a user-defined template, select it in the Save as Template dialog box and click ![delete_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...). |

You must confirm the deletion, since deleting a template is not reversible.

## Distance Measurement Tool

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/visiTag\_distance\_V7-P3\_EN.xml

The Distance Measurement Tool provides two ways to measure the distance.

* **Catheter**: Measures the distance between the real-time ablation catheter tip position and the last VISITAG™ Site that was created. You can also display the distance between the catheter tip and a selected point or VISITAG™ Site within 15 mm.
* **Standard**: Measures the distance between any two selected end points (VISITAG™ Sites, Grid Cells, points, tagged points, or locations on the map). This option is not available during FAM acquisition.

The distance measured is the direct line connecting the center of the object or the catheter tip (not the distance on the map or tissue).

You can activate the tool from the Map Menu or the VISITAG™ Toolbar.

* From the Map, select Distance Measurement - Catheter or Distance Measurement - Standard
* From the toolbar, select Catheter or Standard

In **Standard** mode, click two endpoints. The distance is displayed.

![visitag_siteToSite.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 108: Distance between Two VISITAG™ Site

In **Catheter** mode, the distance between the catheter tip and the last created VISITAG™ Site is automatically displayed. If a new site is created, it becomes the endpoint.

* To measure the distance between the catheter tip and a different VISITAG™ Site, click the site.
* To display the distance between the catheter tip and an EA point, press SHIFT while clicking the point. The point is not selected (map viewers, Point List, or Selected Point Viewer).

In both cases, the object must be within 15 mm of the catheter tip.

![visitag_siteToTip_4-3.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 109: Distance Real-Time Catheter Position and Last VISITAG™ Site

## Area Measurement Tool

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/areaMeasurement\_v7\_EN.xml

The Area Measurement tool provides the area and perimeter of the selected region.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Select Map > Area Measurement.  The Area Measurement dialog box appears.  ![Area_measurement_db.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 2. | Click three or more points on the map that best define the borders of the anatomical structure. When there are three markers, they are joined in a ring. The ring is redrawn with each additional marker.  When the area is marked, the dialog box is updated with the current values.  ![Area_measurement_selected.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 3. | (Optional) To delete one or more markers, right-click the marker and do one of the following:   * Click Delete Marker to delete only the selected marker. * Click Delete All Markers to delete all markers.   The marker or markers are deleted. The Area Measurement dialog box remains open, and you can continue marking points. |
| 4. | (Optional) Click Inverse Area to select everything outside the ring. |
| 5. | Click Clear to clear the selection without closing the dialog box. |

# File Management

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/FileManagement\_EN.xml

CARTO™ 3 Studies can be backed up to external storage media or a network, and then restored to a CARTO™ 3 Workstation for review.

In addition, the Export Data Tool can be used to export several types of data collected during the study.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  It is recommended to use only USB 3.0 devices with NTFS format when backing up or exporting data to improve export time. This configuration is especially recommended when using the Error Reporting Tool. |

## Backing Up, Restoring, and Deleting Studies

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/archiver\_intro\_backupRestore\_c3\_3-2\_EN.xml

REP4611 A-V1-16.1.1

The Study Archiver is used for backing up, restoring, and deleting Studies. Studies and Exported Data can be saved to CD, DVD, USB storage device, or the hospital network.

Data that is backed up to external media is encrypted. Data that is backed up to the hospital network is not encrypted.

Studies can be restored from CARTO™ 3 System V3.1.1 and higher.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Before beginning a backup, take into consideration that the time required to back up a Study depends on the size of the Study and the backup media. For large Studies, at least a few minutes will be required to complete the task. |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| Hospital Network (PACS) | REP4611 security risks V3 tab Sec.14   * If communication to the hospital network is lost during emergency conditions due to failure of the network or physical disconnection, you can use external media to back up or restore data in CARTO™ 3 System.   VSTS 36168: NTF   * The DICOM server option in the Study Archiver is enabled, even if there is no connection to the network. If you try to back up a study to DICOM server, the process starts, but fails after a few minutes.   If the backup process fails, ensure that the Ethernet cable is connected. |
| CD/DVD | * Using medical grade media for backing up studies is recommended. Backups may fail if lower quality media is used. * Rewritable discs are not supported. * When multiple studies are added to the Backup Queue, you will be prompted to insert a new CD or DVD if the Studies do not all fit on a single disc. A single Study cannot be split.   VSTS doc task 197142   * Up to 5.5 GB can be saved to a single DVD. |
| USB Storage Device | 23598, VSTS   * Do not rename backup folders on a USB storage device. If you rename a backup folder, its studies cannot be restored. |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE   * If you want to import Studies for a patient, do this before opening a new study for the same patient. * Some Image Studies that were created in other systems have names that include a period in the Study name. These studies cannot be restored with the same name. Enter a new Study name without periods. |

VSTS 36135: NTF

### Overview of the Study Archiver

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/archiver\_intro\_GUI\_c3\_v7\_EN.xml

![Archiver_fullScreen_backupTab_USB-connected_v7.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 110: Study Archiver

The Study Archiver is divided into the following sections:

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| List of Studies | The list of studies displays all the studies available for backup, delete, or restore (according to the selected tab). An icon appears next to studies that are already backed up. (![Archiver_backupDone_icon_4-2.png](data:image/png;base64...)).  ![Archiver_studyGrid_v4.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| Backup, Delete, or Restore Queue | The Backup Queue, Delete Queue, or Restore Queue (according to the selected tab) displays the studies selected for the current action.  ![Archiver_backupGrid_7.png](data:image/png;base64...)   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE   * To ensure that CARTOREPLAY™ data is exported by the Study Archiver, select the Include CARTOREPLAY™ Module data option when saving the study. This option is available only when saving to USB, because of the large file size. Exporting can take longer than with standard export. * When a CARTO™ 3 Study with one or more attached image studies is moved to the backup queue, the image studies are moved with it. Before backing up, you can remove the image studies from the queue. | |
| Backup To and Restore From | In the Backup To and Restore From sections (the displayed information changes according to the selected tab), you select the appropriate storage medium.  ![Archiver_backupTo_4-3.png](data:image/png;base64...) |

The selection tools are used to prepare the Selected Data list. For multiple selection, press CTRL while clicking the files you want to add or remove.

Table 48: Selection Tools

| Tool | Description |
| --- | --- |
| ![exportItem_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Adds the files selected in the file list to the Selected Data list. |
| ![exportIAll_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Adds all the files in the file list to the Selected Data list. |
| ![export_removeItem_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Removes the selected files from the Selected Data list. |
| ![export_removeAll_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Removes all the files from the Selected Data list. |

### Customizing the Study Archiver

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Archiver\_customizeColumns\_t\_EN.xml

You can customize the display in the Study Archiver by resizing its panes and individual columns, sorting the list of studies by different columns, rearranging the columns in the list of studies, and hiding columns, as described below.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| Arranging Columns | To rearrange columns in the list of studies or the queue, drag the column header to the new location.![Archiver_dragColumn.png](data:image/png;base64...)  Dotted borders appear while you are dragging the column. |
| Displaying Columns | To show or hide columns, click ![arrows_right_single_sml.png](data:image/png;base64...) and then select or clear the columns.![archiver_queue_addCols_menu.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| Resizing Elements | To change the relative size of the list of studies and the queue, drag the divider between them to set the size.  You can resize columns the same way. |
| Sorting | To sort, click the header of the column you want to sort by.![Archiver_headers_sorted.png](data:image/png;base64...)  An arrow appears on the selected column. The arrow points down to indicate descending order or up to indicate ascending order. Click the arrow to reverse the sorting order. |

### Anonymous Backup and Restore

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Archiver\_anonBackup\_c3\_v7\_EN.xml

You can save a copy of a study to CD, DVD, or USB Storage Device with no identifying details. The anonymous backup option applies to all selected Studies in the Backup Queue.

To create an anonymous backup, select Anonymous after you add the Study to the Backup Queue.

![Archiver_backupGrid_anon_v7.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 111: Create Anonymous Backup

After you create an anonymous backup, the source Study will not be marked as backed up in the list of Studies (![Archiver_backupDone_icon_4-2.png](data:image/png;base64...)).

When an anonymous Study is restored, the first and last names are both set to Anonymous\_N, where N is the number. Patient ID is set to 00000.

![Archiver_restore_anon.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 112: Restore Anonymous Backup

### When to Back Up Studies

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/backup\_when\_V7p3\_10\_EN.xml

It is recommended to back up all new patient studies at the end of each procedure or day. To facilitate this, you are prompted to create a backup when exiting a study. In addition, you will be prompted to record the type of arrhythmia. (See ["Exiting a Study"](#target9000072).)

![Exit_study_all_blue.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 113: Exiting a Study

To protect the system, if the hard disk is more than 95% full, features that use space on the hard disk become unavailable (for example, **Continue Study, New Study, Save As, Restore**). When this situation occurs, an alert or error message appears. To create free space on the hard disk, back up patient data, and then delete it from the hard disk drive.

18.1.2.1. V3, 41.1.1

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteCaution.png](data:image/png;base64...) | *CAUTION*  Two copies of patient data should be available for safekeeping. Be sure to back up patient data before deleting it, **because data cannot be recovered, once it has been deleted**. |

18.1.2.2 V3, 41.1.2 , REP4611 A-V4-1.7.4

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteCaution.png](data:image/png;base64...) | *CAUTION*  **Disclaimer**  Biosense Webster WILL HAVE NO LIABILITY FOR LOSS OF DATA from a failed, malfunctioning, or defective Hard Disk Drive of the workstation computer; nor will it be liable FOR PRIVATE DATA LOSS from a locked or defective storage device. |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  These recommendations apply only when backing up study data. For details regarding exported data, see ["Exporting Data"](#target9000073). |

### Backing Up Studies to CD/DVD

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Archiver\_backup\_disc\_c3\_V3-2\_t\_EN.xml

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Place an empty disc in the disc drive. |
| 2. | In the CARTO™ 3 System opening screen, click System.  The System Tools dialog box appears. |
| 3. | Click the Study Archiver button, and then click Backup.  The Study Archiver dialog box appears, with the Backup tab selected. The studies on the Workstation are displayed in the list of studies. |
| 4. | In the Backup To options, select CD/DVD.  The free space available on the disc is displayed. |
| 5. | Add the study or studies to the Backup Queue.  Use the selection tools to add or remove studies from the queue. |
| 6. | Select the appropriate burn rate.   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  The default rate depends on the type of disc. For most types, the fastest rate is optimal. For double-layer discs, a slower speed is recommended. | |
| 7. | Click the Backup button.  The studies being backed up are listed, and a progress bar appears.  ![archiver_backup_progBar.png](data:image/png;base64...) |

When the data has been successfully backed up, the backup queue is cleared and an icon appears next to the backed up studies in the list of studies. (![Archiver_backupDone_icon_4-2.png](data:image/png;base64...))

### Backing Up Studies to USB Storage Device

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Archiver\_backup\_usb\_c3\_V2\_t\_EN.xml

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Data backed up to a USB storage device is not in DICOM format. |

|  |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1. | Connect a USB Storage Device to the CARTO™ 3 Workstation.   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Any USB storage device that is not write-protected must be maintained in accordance with hospital procedures. | |
| 2. | In the CARTO™ 3 System opening screen, click System.  The System Tools dialog box appears. |
| 3. | Click the Study Archiver button, and then click Backup.  The Study Archiver dialog box appears, with the Backup tab selected. The studies on the Workstation are displayed in the list of studies. |
| 4. | In the Backup To options, select USB Storage Device.  The free space available on the device is displayed, along with any folders.   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  If multiple USB storage devices are connected, the device that was connected first is used for the backup. | |
| 5. | Add the study or studies to the Backup Queue.  Use the selection tools to add or remove studies from the queue. |
| 6. | Click the Backup button.  The studies being backed up are listed, and a progress bar appears.  ![archiver_backup_progBar.png](data:image/png;base64...)  When the data has been successfully backed up, the backup queue is cleared and an icon appears next to the backed up studies in the list of studies. (![Archiver_backupDone_icon_4-2.png](data:image/png;base64...)) |
| 7. | Click the Eject button before removing the USB Storage Device. |

### Backing Up Studies to a Network

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Archiver\_backup\_network\_c3\_V4\_EN.xml

If the CARTO™ 3 System is connected to the hospital network, you can back up studies to the network in DICOM format.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | In the CARTO™ 3 System opening screen, click System.  The System Tools dialog box appears. |
| 2. | Click the Study Archiver button, and then click Backup.  The Study Archiver dialog box appears, with the Backup tab selected. The studies on the Workstation are displayed in the list of studies. |
| 3. | In the Backup To options, select the network. |
| 4. | Add the study or studies to the Backup Queue.  Use the selection tools to add or remove studies from the queue. |
| 5. | Click the Backup button.  The studies being backed up are listed, and a progress bar appears.  ![archiver_backup_progBar.png](data:image/png;base64...)  When the data has been successfully backed up, the backup queue is cleared and an icon appears next to the backed up studies in the list of studies. (![Archiver_backupDone_icon_4-2.png](data:image/png;base64...)) |

After you back up studies to the DICOM network, the backup status is indicated by an icon to the right of the study number.

Table 49: Backup Status Indications

| Icon | Indication |
| --- | --- |
| ![Archiver_StorageCommit_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | The study is saved on a DICOM network that does not support storage commitment. |
| ![Archiver_StorageCommit_wait_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | The request for storage commitment is in process. A response has not yet been received. |
| ![Archiver_StorageCommit_pass_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Storage commitment succeeded. Confirmation that the study was successfully saved on the network has been received. |
| ![Archiver_StorageCommit_fail_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Storage commitment failed. |

### Restoring Studies to the Hard Drive from Disc or USB Storage Device

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Archiver\_restore\_fromDisc\_c3\_V2\_t\_EN.xml

Studies can be restored to the CARTO™ 3 Workstation hard drive from CD, DVD, or USB Storage Device.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Insert the disc or USB Storage Device. |
| 2. | In the Opening Screen, click the System button.  The System Tools dialog box appears. |
| 3. | Click the Study Archiver button, and then click Restore.  The Study Archiver dialog box appears, with the Restore tab selected. |
| 4. | In the Restore From Options, select CD/DVD or USB Storage Device.  A list of Studies on the selected medium is displayed. |
| 5. | Add studies to the Restore Queue.  Use the selection tools to add or remove studies from the queue. |
| 6. | Click the Restore button.  The studies being restored are listed, and a progress bar appears. |

When the data has been successfully restored, the Restore Queue is cleared and the restored studies are displayed in the list of studies.

### Restoring Studies to the Hard Drive from Network

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Archiver\_restore\_fromNetwork\_c3\_V2\_t\_EN.xml

Studies can be restored to the CARTO™ 3 Workstation hard drive from the hospital network, if available.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | In the Opening Screen, click the System button.  The System Tools dialog box appears. |
| 2. | Click the Study Archiver button, and then click Restore.  The Study Archiver dialog box appears, with the Restore tab selected. |
| 3. | In the Restore From options, select Network, and then select the appropriate network from the drop-down list.  The Retrieve Network Studies filters appear.  ![Archiver_restore_network_fields.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 4. | Use the filters to find the study:   |  |  | | --- | --- | | Study Date | ![Archiver_restore_network_dateField.png](data:image/png;base64...) | | First Name | ![Archiver_restore_network_FirstNameField.png](data:image/png;base64...) | | Last Name | ![Archiver_restore_network_LastNameField.png](data:image/png;base64...) | | Patient ID | ![Archiver_restore_network_IDField.png](data:image/png;base64...) |  |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Wildcards are supported.   * Use \* to search for any number of characters. * Use ? to search for any single character in a specific location.   For example, enter ?atri\* in the First Name field to find all patients with the first name Patrick, Patricia, or Katrina. | |
| 5. | Click the Query button.  A message is displayed while the query is running. The query may take some time.  When the query is completed, the results are displayed and the message disappears.  ![Archiver_loading_progress.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 6. | Add studies to the Restore Queue.  Use the selection tools to add or remove studies from the queue. |
| 7. | Click the Restore button.  The studies being restored are listed, and a progress bar appears. |

When the data has been successfully restored, the Restore Queue is cleared and the restored studies are displayed in the list of studies.

### Deleting Studies from Hard Drive

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Archiver\_delete\_c3\_V2\_t\_EN.xml

Ensure that you have backed up the data before deleting it from the workstation hard drive. Deleted date that was not backed up *cannot be recovered or restored.*

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | In the CARTO™ 3 System opening screen, click System.  The System Tools dialog box appears. |
| 2. | Click the Study Archiver button, and then click Delete.  The Study Archiver dialog box appears, with the Delete tab selected. All studies on the workstation are displayed in the list of studies. |
| 3. | Add studies to the Delete Queue.  Use the selection tools to add or remove studies from the queue. |
| 4. | Click the Delete button. |
| 5. | If the study was not backed up, you must confirm that you want to delete it.  ![archiver_del_notBackedUp_msg.png](data:image/png;base64...)   * Click Yes to delete the study. * Click No to cancel. |
| 6. | If you have selected all studies associated with a particular patient, a warning appears.  ![delete warning_removePatient.png](data:image/png;base64...)   * Click Yes to delete all patient details as well as the selected studies. * Click No to delete the studies without deleting the patient details. |

The studies being deleted are listed, and a progress bar appears.

## Exporting Data

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/ExportingData\_v7\_EN.xml

The following types of data can be created during a study:

* Screen captures (\*.jpg)
* Video clips (\*.mp4)
* Log files

Raw data can be exported as text files from Review mode (Study > Export Study Data).

**Exporting During a Study:**

Screen captures and video clips can be exported during a Study or from Review Mode (Study > Media Export. All media files associated with the Study are exported to the selected USB storage device.

The data is encrypted and password-protected. You must enter a valid password in order to export the data. A valid password must be between 8 and 15 characters and contain at least 1 number or non-alphanumeric character.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  You cannot perform any other activities while the export is active. |

**Exporting After a Study:**

After a Study, you can use the Export Data Tool to export additional files that are associated with the Study.

By default, the data is encrypted and password-protected. However, if you are exporting only images and/or video clips, you have the option of exporting without encryption.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteDanger.png](data:image/png;base64...) | **WARNING**  If you choose to export the data without this protection, you must acknowledge that you may be liable for failing to appropriately safeguard this data (e.g., if your device is lost, stolen, or hacked) or if you fail to comply with applicable laws and regulations relating to the privacy of medical records and personal information. |

Be sure to save the password securely. If the password is lost, export the data again. **If the study is no longer available, there is no way to recover the content.**

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  The CARTO™ 3 System uses the FFMpeg tool to compress the video captures to MPEG 4 format before exporting them (FFMpeg is open source software licensed under the GNU General Public License (GPL) Version 2 or later).  The FFMpeg source files and license information is available with each CARTO™ 3 System. |

### Export Data Window

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/exportTool\_db\_v6\_EN.xml

The Export Data window is accessed from the System Tools dialog box.

![exportData_imageSelected_V7.png](data:image/png;base64...)

The following table describes the sections of the Export Data window.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| Data Encryption | Encrypts and password-protects the data before export.  If the Include Log Files check box is not selected, you can export without encryption. You must confirm your responsibility for safeguarding the data and complying with applicable laws and regulations.  ![exportData_DataEncryption_V6.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| Study List | Lists the studies available for selection.  Display data by study date or by patient name.  ![exportData_displayOptions_v4.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| File List | Displays all the exportable files associated with the selected study.  ![exportData_selection_v4.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| Selected Data List | Lists the files selected for export  ![exportData_selectedData_v6.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| Preview Pane | Previews the file selected in the file list.  ![exportTool_preview_db_6p5.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| Export Target Pane | Used for selecting the export target: CD, DVD, USB storage device, or the network (where available).  The information displayed depends on the selected target.  ![exportData_exportTarget_v4.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| Export Details | Used for starting the export process and monitoring the progress.  ![exportData_status_v4.png](data:image/png;base64...) |

The selection tools are used to prepare the Selected Data list. For multiple selection, press CTRL while clicking the files you want to add or remove.

Table 50: Selection Tools

| Tool | Description |
| --- | --- |
| ![exportItem_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Adds the files selected in the file list to the Selected Data list. |
| ![exportIAll_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Adds all the files in the file list to the Selected Data list. |
| ![export_removeItem_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Removes the selected files from the Selected Data list. |
| ![export_removeAll_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Removes all the files from the Selected Data list. |

#### Exporting Screen Captures and Video Clips

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/export\_media\_files\_c3\_6p5\_EN.xml

You can use the Export Data Tool to export screen captures and video clips that were acquired during a study.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Exit the study. |
| 2. | In the CARTO™ 3 System opening screen, click the System button.  ![openingScreen_system.png](data:image/png;base64...)  The System Tools dialog box appears. |
| 3. | Click Export Data.  The Export Data Window appears. |
| 4. | In the Patient Name column, select a patient.  All the studies for that patient are listed in the Study - Date column. |
| 5. | Select a study.  The related files appear in the file list. In the Type column, screen captures (\*.jpg) are listed as Snapshot file. Video clips (\*.mp4) are listed as Movie file. |
| 6. | Select the files you want to export.  When an item is selected, you can view it in the Preview pane.  ![exportTool_preview_db_6p5.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 7. | Click ![exportItem_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) to add the selected file to the Selected Data list. Click ![exportIAll_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...) to add all files. |
| 8. | Enter a valid password for Data Encryption.  If you are exporting only images and/or video, you can clear the Data Encryption check box. |
| 9. | In the export target pane, select the export option:   * CD/DVD: Export the data to a CD or DVD.   Enter a name for the disk, and click Burn.  Click Open Tray to eject the disk.   * USB: Export the data to a USB storage device.   If there are folders on the device, you can browse to a different folder. You can create new folders if needed.  Click the Export button to export the data. |

A progress bar indicates the status. When the export process is finished, a check mark appears.

![exportData_status_v4.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 114: Indication of Successful Data Export

See also:

* ["Creating Video Clips and Screen Captures"](#target9000074)
* ["Export Data Window"](#target9000075)

#### Exporting Study Data as Text Files

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/export\_create\_textData\_c3\_v7\_EN.xml

You can export Study data as text files that can be used in a program such as Microsoft Excel. For base CARTO™ 3 System data, the data includes the following:

* For each socket: Location of all electrodes
* For each map: Two raw data files (<map\_name> .mesh and <map\_name>\_car.txt)
* For each ablation session: One raw data file including power, impedance, and temperature over time

Data can also be exported for the following modules:

* CARTO VISITAG™ Module
* PASO™ Module
* CARTOFINDER™ Module (Where the CARTO PRIME™ Module is installed.)

The data is exported in a \*.zip file. The file name is in the following format: Export\_<Study Name>\_<Study Date>.zip. The file is available through the Export Data tool.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | In Review Mode, click Study > Export Study Data.  The Export Study Data window opens.  ![ExportStudyData_DialogBox_v7.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 2. | Click Export.  The data files are exported. |
| 3. | Exit the study. |
| 4. | In the CARTO™ 3 System opening screen, click the System button.  ![openingScreen_system.png](data:image/png;base64...)  The System Tools dialog box appears. |
| 5. | Click Export Data.  The Export Data Window appears. |
| 6. | Select the Study. |
| 7. | In the file list, select the exported Raw Data file.  The file name is in the format Export\_<Study Name>\_<Study Date>.zip. You can view file details in the Preview pane. |
| 8. | Click ![exportItem_icon.png](data:image/png;base64...).  The file is added to the Selected Data list. |
| 9. | Enter a valid password for Data Encryption, then confirm the password. |
| 10. | In the export target pane, select the export option:   * CD/DVD: Export the data to a CD or DVD.   Enter a name for the disk, and click Burn.  Click Open Tray to eject the disk.   * USB: Export the data to a USB storage device.   If there are folders on the device, you can browse to a different folder. You cannot create new folders.  Click the Export button to export the data. |

A progress bar indicates the status. When the export process is finished, a check mark appears.

![exportData_status_v4.png](data:image/png;base64...)

See also:

* ["Exporting Data"](#target9000076)

### Exporting Media Files During Study

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/export\_media\_files\_7p2\_EN.xml

During a Study, you can export image and video files.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Click Study > Export Media.  The Media Export window appears.  ![media_export.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 2. | Select the USB storage device. |
| 3. | Enter a password.  A valid password must be between 8 and 15 characters and contain at least 1 number or non-alphanumeric character. |
| 4. | Confirm the password.  Be sure to save the password securely. If the password is lost, export the data again. **If the study is no longer available, there is no way to recover the content.** |
| 5. | Click Export. |

The System exports the files to the selected USB storage device.

During the export process, you cannot do anything else. If necessary, press Cancel.

## Error Reporting Tool

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/ERT\_intro\_V7\_EN.xml

If an error occurs on the CARTO™ 3 System, report the error to Biosense Webster. Use the Error Reporting tool in the Lightning application to collect anonymized data about the study or System. This data will help Biosense Webster personnel identify the cause of the error and correct it.

The Error Reporting Tool has two different flows, depending on when the error occurred.

* If the error occurred during a Study or in Review mode, select the specific study.
* If the error occurred before initialization or in application tools, select the date that the error occurred.

The exported data is encrypted. There are two encryption options:

* **Automatic encryption**: Use this option when sending details to Biosense Webster for error reporting. (Default)
* **Password protection**: Use this option when exporting data for personal use. The exported data cannot be used for error reporting.

If you choose the Password protection option, you must define a valid password.

Be sure to save the password securely. If the password is lost, export the data again. **If the study is no longer available, there is no way to recover the content.**

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  It is recommended to use only USB 3.0 devices with NTFS format when backing up or exporting data to improve export time. This configuration is especially recommended when using the Error Reporting Tool. |

### Reporting Error During Study

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/ErrorType\_v7\_EN.xml

Follow this procedure for reporting errors that occurred during a study.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | In the CARTO™ 3 System opening screen, click System.  The System Tools dialog box appears. |
| 2. | Click Error Reporting.  The Error Reporting window appears. |
| 3. | Select Error occurred during study (after 'initialize') or while in 'Review Study'.  ![error_during_study.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 4. | If relevant, add details in the Error Description text box and enter the Complaint Number (PC number) that you received when initially reporting the error. |
| 5. | Click Next.  A list of studies appears.  ![error_during_study_select.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 6. | Select the relevant study or studies and then click Next.  The Export screen appears, with a list of files to be exported in the error report.  ![error_during_study_export.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 7. | If available, select additional files that you want to include (video clips and screen captures).  You can display any of these files in the preview pane by clicking the line of the file. |
| 8. | Connect a USB storage device to the Workstation.  The USB storage device (with its folders) is listed in the Error Reporting window. |
| 9. | Select the USB storage device and target folder for the export.  You can add a new folder by right-clicking in the folder pane. |
| 10. | If you want the Workstation to shut down automatically after the export, select the check box.  The estimated size of the collected data appears at the bottom of the window. If there is not enough free space on the USB storage device, a message appears. |
| 11. | Select the required encryption option:   * **Automatic encryption** – Use this option when sending details to Biosense Webster for error reporting. (Default) * Password protection – If you are exporting the data for personal use, define a valid password. When prompted, confirm the password. A valid password must be between 8 and 15 characters and contain at least 1 number or non-alphanumeric character.   ![ERT_Export_Password.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...) |
| 12. | Click Export.  The progress bar shows the progress of the export.  ![ERT_Progress.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...) |
| 13. | When the export is completed (green check mark appears), click Exit.  ![ERT_complet.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...) |

# Security

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/security\_intro\_7p4\_EN.xml

The CARTO™ 3 System is designed to connect securely to a variety of networks. CARTO™ 3 System security is in accordance with the IEC 80001.1 standard.

CARTO™ 3 System security includes:

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| Operating system security | Microsoft® Windows® 10 |
| Firewall protection | Microsoft® Windows® 10 firewall application  Enabled by default. |
| Secure Login | Prevents unauthorized access  Disabled by default. |
| Antivirus protection | Trellix antivirus application  Enabled by default. |

To ensure security and patient privacy, follow these recommendations:

* Enable the secure login feature for password-protected access
* Enforce audit policies
* Enforce use of user management system
* Use strong passwords
* Change passwords regularly
* Ensure that the CARTO™ 3 System is operated only in a secured hospital environment

REP4611 security risks V3 tab Sec.14

If communication to the hospital network is lost during emergency conditions due to failure of the network or physical disconnection, you can use external media to back up or restore data in CARTO™ 3 System.

## Antivirus

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/antivirus\_intro\_EN.xml

The System is protected against security threats and attacks from infected external sources by both an antivirus application and an Allow List. The antivirus application is updated automatically or manually, using antivirus definition files. The CARTO™ 3 Allow List mechanism ensures only approved applications can execute on the CARTO™ 3 Workstation. By default, all other applications are denied execution.

The antivirus definition files can be upgraded automatically or manually. For antivirus definition files to be updated automatically the hospital must configure their network to securely allow communication with Trellix, the antivirus application provider.

* Configure the System for the antivirus application to run automatically. Antivirus definition files are accessed through the hospital network using DICOM connectivity configurations.
* Update manually with updated antivirus definition files via a USB storage device.

You can test connectivity from the System to the Trellix antivirus definition update website using the Test button on the DICOM Network Configuration page, the result appears to the right of the button.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DICOM_AVTest_success.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Indicates connectivity is present. |
| ![DICOM_AVTest_fail.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Indicates no connectivity. |
| ![DICOM_AVTest_notdone.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Indicates a test has not been performed. |

To prevent a negative impact on performance, the System does not start scheduled scans while a study is in progress. If a study is started while a scan is in progress, the scan will stop.

### Virus Scan upon Exiting

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Scan\_and\_shutDown\_EN.xml

When the antivirus application is enabled, the Exit dialog box includes an additional option: Scan & Shut Down.

![exit_dialog_av_6p5.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 115: Exit Dialog Box when Antivirus Application Enabled

When Scan & Shut Down is selected, a scan for viruses is performed. A progress bar is displayed, showing the progress of the scan.

After completion of the scan, the workstation shuts down.

### Unremovable Viruses

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/virusDetectedWarning\_3.2\_EN.xml

If the antivirus application detects a virus during a scan of the System, it automatically attempts to remove the virus and repair the infected file without prompting the user for action.

However, if the antivirus application cannot remove a virus, additional action is required. The opening screen, when it next appears, displays a message recommending not to use the System until Biosense Webster Service and Support Department resolves the issue. The New Study, Image Integration, and Review Study buttons are disabled when the message appears.

![VirusDetected_v3.2.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 116: Unremovable Virus Warning

To continue using the System without resolving the issue, the user must select the check box in the message. All buttons are then enabled.

The message is displayed each time the opening screen appears until the issue is resolved.

### Viruses on USB Storage Devices

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/virusDetectedWarning\_USB\_EN.xml

Connected USB storage devices are scanned only when files are being accessed. When a file with a virus that cannot be removed is detected, a message appears, notifying the user that the file cannot be accessed. The antivirus blocks access to the infected file to protect the operating system.

![VirusDetectedUSB.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 117: System Warning when Virus Detected on USB Storage Device

To continue working, click OK.

## Firewall

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/firewall\_intro\_EN.xml

When enabled, the firewall application blocks unauthorized network access to the CARTO™ 3 System. The firewall application does not affect user workflow.

## Secure Login

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/HIPAA\_intro\_V8p3\_EN.xml

The CARTO™ 3 System complies with all health regulations regarding privacy and integrity by providing a secure login to the system. When enabled, users are required to sign into the CARTO™ 3 System with a username and password. Secure Login records an audit trail file containing the username, time and date of logins, and system event descriptions. When using Secure Login, the system will logoff automatically and return the user to the Login dialogue box after a user-defined idle time is reached.

The audit trail file of system events is available for export by the hospital CARTO™ 3 System Administrator (OR Admin). The audit trail lists system events, identifying them with the following information:

* Time and date
* Username
* Event description.

The CARTO™ 3 System has four levels of application access, which allows selective access to Dialogs and Menus, as shown in the following table.

Table 51: User Types and Access Levels

| User Type | Role Associated with User Type | Access Level |
| --- | --- | --- |
| OR Admin | The Operating Room Admin (OR Admin) is a hospital administrator for the CARTO™ 3 System | User Management for the local Workstation with access to the CARTO™ 3 System and no access to Technician Tools |
| OR User | The Operating Room User (OR User) is an EP Doctor, or other EP staff | Access to the CARTO™ 3 System only, with no access to Technician Tools or User Management |
| CAS | BW Clinical Account Specialist (CAS) | Access to the CARTO™ 3 System, User Management, and partial access to Technician Tools (Upgrade and CAS Tools tabs only) |
| FSE | BW Field System Engineer (FSE) | Access to the CARTO™ 3 System, User Management, and Technician Tools for system configuration and software upgrades |

The CARTO™ 3 System has a User Management feature to manage individual user accounts. Once activated, user account management activities, including creating and disabling login accounts, will be performed by the customer.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  The passwords for user types FSE and CAS are confidential and should not be disclosed to users of the CARTO™ 3 System. They are to be used by Biosense Webster representatives only. |

### Logging onto the CARTO™ 3 System

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/HIPAA\_Logon\_v8p3\_EN.xml

When the secure login feature is enabled, each time the CARTO™ 3 System starts or is reloaded the Login dialog box is displayed.

![HIPAA_login_all_v8p3.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...)

Figure 118: Secure Login Options

|  | Login Method and Password Management | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Login with Password | Enter your Username and Password, click Login. The CARTO™ 3 System opens. |
| 2 | Login with one-time code | Only available for CAS and FSE user types. |
| 3 | Forgot Password? | Click Forgot Password? and the List of Administrators dialog appears. Contact a named OR Admin to reset your password. |
| 4 | ![power_button.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Select the power button icon and the Exit dialog box displays. |
| 5 | Change Password | When you select Change Password the Login dialog box expands.  To change your password complete all available options, then click Apply.  For more details about changing your password, see ["Changing the Password"](#target9000077). |
| 6 | Login with Limited Access | In an emergency situation you can log into the CARTO™ 3 System without a password, click the Limited Access button.  The CARTO™ 3 System opens with limited functionality. You can start a study, but cannot access functions that use patient information.  For full CARTO™ 3 System functionality, login with your username and password. |

### Changing the Password

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/HIPAA\_ChangePassword\_EN.xml

For security reasons, you are strongly advised to change your password when logging into the system for the first time. To change your password you must know your current password. If you have forgotten your password, contact one of the CARTO™ 3 System Administrators. For a List of Administrators click Forgot Password.

To change your password:

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | In the Login dialog box, click Change Password.  The Login dialog box expands.  Type your username in the Username field.  ![Change_password_applyy.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...) |
| 2. | Type your current password in the Old Password field. |
| 3. | Type a new password in the New Password field.  Passwords must meet the following requirements:   * Be between 8 and 15 characters in length * Contain at least 1 number and 1 non-alphanumeric character * Current password cannot be reused * Three previous passwords cannot be reused |
| 4. | In the Confirm Password field, type the new password again. |
| 5. | Click Apply.  If the criteria for changing your password are not met an error message appears with the point of failure detail.  When the criteria are met, the password is set to the new password. |

### User Management Functions

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/User\_Mgmt\_OR\_Admin\_EN.xml

OR Admin users are responsible for creating and managing the local machine for CARTO™ 3 System users.

Access to user management and administration functionality is through the System Tools > User Management dialog box.

The User Management dialog box opens with two tabs: User Administration and System Options.

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/HIPAA\_user\_admin\_tab\_EN.xml

Use the User Administration tab for the following tasks:

* Adding and removing user types: OR Admin and OR User
* Enabling and disabling access for user types: OR Admin and OR User
* Resetting user passwords

First Name and Last Name must contain 1-20 characters.  Password must meet the following requirements:   * Be between 8 and 15 characters in length * Contain at least 1 number and 1 non-alphanumeric character * Current password cannot be reused * Three previous passwords cannot be reused   To assign administration responsibilities, select OR Admin.  Complete all fields correctly, then click OK. A success message appears. |

#### System Options Tab

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/HIPAA\_system\_options\_EN.xml

Use the System Options tab for the following tasks:

* Exporting System Settings, including user details

![user_mgmt_sys_options_allow.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...)

Figure 120: System Options Tab

|  | Section | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |

When Secure Login is enabled for the first time a message appears with the option to create an OR Admin user. You should add a new user, and select the OR Admin check box option at this time.  When the option is disabled a warning message appears. |
| 2 | Password Expiration | When secure login is enabled, the OR Admin can set an expiration period for passwords.  In the Password expiration after check box, select a time frame from the drop-down menu.  When the option is enabled the current password will expire at the end of the selected time frame. |
| 3 | Automatic Logoff | When secure login is enabled, the OR Admin can set a specific time frame for the system to automatically log off when the system is on the opening screen.  In the Automatic log off after check box select a time frame from the drop down menu.  When the option is enabled the System will automatically log off at the end of the selected time frame. |

In the Automatic log off after check box select a number from the drop-down menu.  When the option is enabled the System will prevent access to the user.  When a user is unable to access the System, an OR Admin can reinstate the user. |
| 5 | Apply | Changes to System Options are accepted when you click Apply. |

| 7 | Export Audit Trail | You can export an audit trail to review system events. For example, user login and logout, patient data modification, study opening and closing, backup, restore, deletion of studies, data export and user management modifications.  The audit log files are part of Windows Event Logs and limited to a maximum size of 100 MB. When the event log file reaches the maximum size, new entries override older entries.  Export audit trail data from the System Options tab by clicking the Export Audit Trail button. The Export Data dialog opens.  Select Carto System > System > CARTO\_Audit\_Trail.evtx file.  ![Export_audit_trail_v8.png](data:image/png;base64...)  Figure 121: Audit Trail File  Make sure the media chosen for your export (CD/DVD or USB) is available.  In the Export to: section, select the media, then click Export.  For information about exporting data, see["Export Data Window"](#target9000078). |
| 8 | Import System Users | The OR Admin can import system users.  When importing system users, all existing local users are deleted.  To import system users, click Import System Users. When the Import System Users dialog appears, select an import method: USB or DVD.  CAS and FSE users can login using a QR code. |

### Installation and Upgrade

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/HIPAA\_Installation\_andUpgrade\_EN.xml

Following a new installation, when Secure Login is disabled, the System will include the following predefined users: FSE, CAS, and Limited Access.

When Secure Login is enabled, FSE and CAS users can access the System using a QR code. All user types can then be created locally.

During an upgrade, all defined users and passwords are preserved.

# ACCURESP™ Module

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/About\_RER\_EN.xml

The ACCURESP™ Module for Respiration Gating enables the CARTO™ 3 System to learn the patient's respiratory motion and train for it. This training allows the user to set a threshold for selective acquisition of location data. When EA points and FAM data are acquired within this threshold (from the end expiration period), heart movement due to respiration is minimal.

The ACCURESP™ Module can be activated or deactivated before or during a clinical procedure as desired.

## Supported Catheters

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/RER\_catheters\_v7p5\_EN.xml

Respiration Gating Training can be performed with any magnetic sensor based catheter.[[22]](#footnote-22)

See also:

* ["Respiration Gating Training"](#target9000079)

## Respiration Gating Options in the CARTO™ 3 System Interface

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/changes\_toGUI\_v6\_EN.xml

The following features and options in the CARTO™ 3 System Interface relate to Respiration Gating:

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ACCURESP™ Module Menu | The ACCURESP™ Module menu command appears in the Tools menu. It opens a submenu with options relating to Respiration Gating. |
| Respiration Gating Window | Initially, the Respiration Gating Window is used to set the training parameters and start respiration training. During training, the window displays a progress bar. After training, the window displays a graph showing respiration amplitude change over time. The graph is color-coded to indicate when the respiratory cycle is within the Respiration Gating Threshold (orange is within the gating threshold; yellow is above the gating threshold). |
| FAM Toolbar | The Respiration Filter option enables or disables the Respiration-Filtered FAM acquisition mode. When this acquisition mode is enabled, FAM location data (volume) is not acquired for data above the Respiration Gating Threshold. When the mode is disabled, all location data is acquired. However, you can choose to hide or show data acquired above the Respiration Gating Threshold. |
| Map Shortcut Menu | The Exclude respiration data option in the Map shortcut (right-click) menu hides or shows FAM data that was acquired above the Respiration Gating Threshold. When enabled, FAM location data that was acquired above the Respiration Gating Threshold is not displayed in the FAM volume or reconstruction.  This option is selected by default. |
| Acquisition Filters | Respiration Gated Acquisition mode can be enabled through the Acquisition Filters tab of the Preferences dialog box. When this option is enabled, the System acquires EA points only when they are annotated and within the Respiration Gating Threshold. |
| Point List | Electroanatomical (EA) Points are labeled ![PtList_inThreshold.png](data:image/png;base64...) (acquired when the respiratory cycle was within the Respiration Gating Threshold) or ![PtList_outThreshold.png](data:image/png;base64...) (acquired when the respiratory cycle was above the Respiration Gating Threshold) in the Respiration (![PtList_RespColumn.png](data:image/png;base64...)) column of the Point List. |
| Beat Buffer | The Beat Buffer is color-coded to indicate whether the data was acquired within or above the Respiration Gating Threshold (orange is within the gating threshold; yellow is above the gating threshold). |
| CARTOSOUND™ Module | Gating status is displayed for frames and contours.[[23]](#footnote-23) |

## Preparing for a Study with Respiration Gating

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/preparations\_EN.xml

### Respiration Gating Training

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/respiration\_training\_v6\_EN.xml

REP4611 A-V1-1.30.4

During the training phase (up to 40 seconds), a supported catheter is placed in the heart. The catheter should be in a fixed location on the endocardium, in an area with a large respiratory swing.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  When using a SOUNDSTAR™ Catheter for Respiration Gating Training, place the catheter tip on the coronary sinus ostium (CS Os) or slightly inside the proximal part of the CS. |

For a ventilated patient, you can select Mechanical ventilation before training. When this option is active, point acquisition in Respiration Gated Acquisition Mode is faster. (For information regarding this mode, see ["EA Points and Respiration Gating"](#target9000080)).

![rgw_startTraining_v6.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 122: Respiration Gating Dialog Box: Start Training

After the training period, the Respiration Graph is displayed in the Respiration Gating window (see ["Respiration Graph"](#target9000081)).

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  REP4611 v3 tab, 1.1.1.7   * Do not touch the patches during Respiration Gating training.   If patches must be touched during training to fix patch adherence, restart training after adjusting the patches.   * Respiration Gating training will fail if the training catheter is disconnected during training. |

Retraining is required in the following situations:

* After System initialization is performed
* After resetting catheter visualization
* When a patch error occurs
* When an alert and/or system message appears, indicating that training is required
* When Respiration Gating Training fails

If a chest or back patch is disconnected during a Study with Respiration Gating, the displayed gating status will be incorrect.

If this occurs:

1. Reconnect the patch.
2. Initialize catheter visualization.
3. Repeat Respiration Gating Training.

![Train.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 123: Respiration Gating Training System Message

![rgw_trainingFailed_slip_v6.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 124: Respiration Gating Training Failure: Stabilization

![rgw_trainingFailed_movement_v6.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 125: Respiration Gating Training Notification: Insufficient Movement

### Activating Respiration Gating

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/activate\_RER\_v6\_EN.xml

Respiration Gating can be enabled or disabled.

|  |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1. | Click Tools > ACCURESP™ Module > Enable Respiration Gating.  You will be prompted to perform respiration gating training.  ![Train.png](data:image/png;base64...)   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  If you do not perform respiration gating training at this stage, you can perform training later. Click Tools > ACCURESP™ Module > Train Respiration Gating, and then follow steps below. | |
| 2. | Click Train.  The Respiration Gating window appears.  ![rgw_startTraining_v6.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 3. | Stabilize a navigation catheter in contact with the wall of the heart in the chamber to be mapped. |
| 4. | For a ventilated patient, select Mechanical ventilation.  When this option is active, point acquisition in Respiration Gated Acquisition Mode is faster. |
| 5. | In the Connector list, select the socket the catheter is connected to. |
| 6. | Click Start Training.  The progress bar appears.  ![rgw_trainingProgress_v6.png](data:image/png;base64...)   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  You can stop respiration training at any time and continue working without Respiration Gating. | |

When Respiration Gating Training ends, the Respiration Graph is displayed.

![rgw_graph_v6.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 126: Respiration Gating Window after Successful Training

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  If you want this window to close automatically after successful training, select Tools > Auto-close Training window upon success. Your selection can be saved to a user-defined template. |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteCaution.png](data:image/png;base64...) | *CAUTION*  When Mechanical ventilation was selected during training and Respiration Gated Mode is active, points are acquired within one respiratory cycle based on a preliminary gating value. After a second respiratory cycle, the gating status is confirmed.  If Mechanical ventilation Mode is used for a non-ventilated patient, the gating status for points acquired may change after the confirmation process. Points that were marked with ![PtList_inThreshold.png](data:image/png;base64...) change to ![PtList_outThreshold.png](data:image/png;base64...) in the Point List. Evaluate these points carefully. |

### Respiration Gating Threshold

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/set\_gatting\_v6\_EN.xml

The Respiration Gating Threshold marks the preferred acquisition phase of the respiratory cycle. FAM data and EA Points are labeled according to whether they were acquired within the threshold or above this threshold. The threshold is set by the System.

REP4611 v3 tab, 1.1.1.5, v2 tab 1.3.1.5

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteCaution.png](data:image/png;base64...) | *CAUTION*  If the patient's respiration rate rises to 30 breaths per minute or higher, the ACCURESP™ Module will not function correctly. If this occurs, continue to work according to current practice, without using the ACCURESP™ Module. |

REP4611 D-V2-1.3.1.1

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  A temporary change to the respiration pattern might lead to incorrect gating. If such a temporary change in respiration is noted, treat automatically-acquired points or volume according to current practice. |

### Respiration Graph

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/view\_realTimeGraph\_v6\_EN.xml

The Respiration Gating window displays a real-time graph showing the respiration amplitude change over time. Time scale and amplitudes can be adjusted by using the arrow keys.

If the window is closed, click Tools > ACCURESP™ Module > Graphs Viewer Respiration Graph

The graph is color coded:

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| Yellow | Above the gating threshold |
| Orange | Within the gating threshold |
| Gray | The current respiratory cycle has not ended, so the calculation to determine if this data is above or below threshold has not been performed. The status will be updated at the end of the respiratory cycle. |

The Respiration Graph can be used as an indicator for acquiring points within the gating threshold.

![rgw_graph_v6.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 127: Respiration Graph

VSTS 36415: NTF

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Ensure that the Respiration Graph is not positioned over a toolbar. If the Respiration Graph is positioned over a toolbar, the graph display might be distorted. |

## Mapping with Respiration Gating

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/rer\_mapping\_EN.xml

Respiration Gating is based on data measured during a full respiratory cycle. Determining whether data is within the threshold or above it requires that the respiratory cycle has finished.

The FAM reconstruction is colored and EA point data are labeled according to their gating status, so data above the threshold can be excluded or included as desired.

This respiration gating status is retained when performing Save Map As or Remap.

### FAM Reconstructions and Respiration Gating

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/rer\_famReconstructions\_v6p5\_EN.xml

During FAM acquisition, all location data is acquired, even if it is above the Respiration Gating Threshold. You can control the display of the data above the threshold.

* Manual exclusion mode (default)

The volume that was acquired within the gating threshold is colored orange and volume that was acquired above the threshold is colored yellow. The yellow volume can be included in the map, or permanently deleted with a special mode of the FAM Eraser Tool.

* Automatic exclusion mode

Volume that was acquired above the threshold is excluded from the map display.

You can switch between the manual and automatic FAM exclusion modes by selecting Exclude respiration data in the Map shortcut menu. Changing modes clears the FAM regional resolution level.

If Exclude respiration data is enabled, it is indicated in the Map List.

![map_list_RER.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 128: Map List when Exclude respiration data Is Enabled

#### Erasing FAM Volume Acquired Above the Threshold

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/erase\_outOfThreshold\_FAM\_EN.xml

When a FAM map is built in Acquire All mode, you can manually exclude (hide) location data that was acquired above the Respiration Gating Threshold.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Acquire a new FAM map and show the volume (![fam_showVolume.png](data:image/png;base64...)).  Volume acquired within the threshold is orange; volume acquired above the threshold is yellow.  ![FAMvolume.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 2. | Activate the Eraser tool (![FAMeraser.png](data:image/png;base64...)).  A circle appears around the cursor.  ![FAMvolumeCircle.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 3. | Adjust the size of the circle by rotating the mouse wheel. |
| 4. | Right-click and drag the cursor over the area you want to remove.  The yellow volume under the tip of the cursor is removed.   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Left-click will remove all FAM data under the tip of the cursor.  ![FAMvolumeErase.png](data:image/png;base64...) | |
| 5. | Continue until you have the required shape. |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  The volume above the gating threshold can be removed in layers. Use CTRL + right-click to remove a cylindrical volume, 2 mm high, with each click. Use SHIFT + CTRL + right-click to remove a layer, 2 mm deep, along the surface under the Eraser tool. |

#### Automatically Exclude Data Acquired Above the Threshold

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/autoDelete\_outOfThreshold\_FAM\_EN.xml

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Start a new FAM map. |
| 2. | Right-click the map and select Exclude respiration data. |
| 3. | Start recording. |

At the end of the cycle, the volume within the gating threshold changes to orange. Volume above the threshold is excluded from the displayed map.

The next time you begin a map, it returns to manual deletion mode with the last threshold settings.

### EA Points and Respiration Gating

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/rer\_eaPoints\_6-3\_EN.xml

When EA points are acquired with the ACCURESP™ Module active, the Respiration (![PtList_RespColumn.png](data:image/png;base64...)) column of the Point List indicates the gating status of each point.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![PtList_inThreshold.png](data:image/png;base64...) | The point was acquired within the Respiration Gating threshold |
| ![PtList_outThreshold.png](data:image/png;base64...) | The point was acquired above the Respiration Gating threshold |
| ![PtList_NA.png](data:image/png;base64...) | The ACCURESP™ Module was not active when the point was acquired, or the Respiration Gating status calculation has not ended. The status will be updated at the end of the calculation. |

![RcolumnPtList.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 129: Respiration Column of the Point List

If the Respiration column is hidden, click ![PtListArrow.png](data:image/png;base64...) and select Respiration from the list.

When points are copied or moved to another map, they retain the ![PtList_inThreshold.png](data:image/png;base64...) or ![PtList_outThreshold.png](data:image/png;base64...) labels.

The Beat Buffer also indicates the gating status. If the calculation has not ended, beats are white.

![beatBuffer_rer_extended.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 130: Respiration Gating Status in the Beat Buffer

In Respiration Gated Acquisition Mode, the System acquires EA points only when they are within the Respiration Gating Threshold. This option is enabled through the Acquisition Filters tab of the Preferences dialog box. When this option is enabled, the System waits for the first annotation that is also within the Respiration Gating Threshold every time the EA acquisition button is clicked. You can temporarily override the feature, if needed, by clicking the button again.

For a ventilated patient, you can select Mechanical ventilation before training. When this option is active, point acquisition in Respiration Gated Acquisition Mode is faster.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteCaution.png](data:image/png;base64...) | *CAUTION*  When Mechanical ventilation was selected during training and Respiration Gated Mode is active, points are acquired within one respiratory cycle based on a preliminary gating value. After a second respiratory cycle, the gating status is confirmed.  If Mechanical ventilation Mode is used for a non-ventilated patient, the gating status for points acquired may change after the confirmation process. Points that were marked with ![PtList_inThreshold.png](data:image/png;base64...) change to ![PtList_outThreshold.png](data:image/png;base64...) in the Point List. Evaluate these points carefully. |

#### Selecting Multiple Points

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/select\_multiple\_pts\_EN.xml

You can select all points with the same gating status.

|  |  |

| 1. | Right-click a point in the Point List or the Map.  The Point shortcut menu appears. |
| 2. | Click Select and then choose one of the following:   * Within respiratory threshold * Above respiratory threshold |

All points with the selected gating status are selected, in both the Point List and the Main Map Viewer.

#### Automatically Acquire EA Points Within the Threshold

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/autoGate\_eaPts\_v7-5\_EN.xml

The system can be set to acquire EA points only when they are within the Respiration Gating Threshold.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Click Tools > Preferences.  The Preferences dialog box appears. |
| 2. | In the Acquisition Filters tab, select Respiration Gated.   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  If N/A appears next to the Respiration Gated option, training has not yet completed successfully. Once training has occurred, the N/A will no longer appear. |   ![continuous_acqu_filters_db_sys_7p5.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 3. | Click OK (OK) to save the change and close the dialog box. |
| 4. | Click the EA acquisition button of the Acquisition panel.  The button turns orange to show that acquisition is in progress. The button remains this way until a point is acquired.  ![acquire_RER_gatedEA_frozen_v6.png](data:image/png;base64...)  This can continue for up to two respiratory cycles. During this time, keep the catheter in the same location until the point is acquired. |

Point data is acquired when the System finds an annotation within the threshold. The Acquisition Panel returns to its normal state.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  To override this mode and acquire a point immediately, click the orange EA acquisition button. The point will be acquired, even if it is not within the Respiration Gating Threshold. Respiration Gated acquisition mode remains active. |

### Respiration Gating and the CARTOSOUND™ Module

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/rer\_cartoSound\_EN.xml

Respiration Gating Status can be displayed in the CARTOSOUND™ Module.[[24]](#footnote-24) For availability, contact the Biosense Webster Service and Support Department.
